### Intercom
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/10963144-understanding-stripe-payouts-with-bsport-a-complete-guide

Managing payouts efficiently is essential for businesses using Bsport and Stripe Connect. As a Bsport client, your transactions are processed through Stripe, and payouts are sent based on a structured payout schedule. This guide explains:
Bsport and Stripe Connect
Bsport client
a structured payout schedule
✅ How payouts work
How payouts work
✅ Processing times for different payment methods
Processing times for different payment methods
✅ Payout schedules (daily, weekly, and monthly)
Payout schedules (daily, weekly, and monthly)
✅ How refunds & disputes impact your payouts
How refunds & disputes impact your payouts
✅ How to track your payouts easily
How to track your payouts easily

## 🔹 1. How Do Stripe Payouts Work with Bsport?

🔹 1. How Do Stripe Payouts Work with Bsport?
When a customer pays for a service or membership at your business, the funds do not go directly to your bank account. Instead, they go through a Stripe Connect Account, which we manage on your behalf.
do not go directly to your bank account
Stripe Connect Account
💡 Bsport handles all Stripe transactions and manages your payouts.
Bsport handles all Stripe transactions and manages your payouts.

## ⚙️ The Payout Process:

⚙️ The Payout Process:
1️⃣ Customer makes a payment (via credit card, Apple Pay, Google Pay, SEPA, etc.).
Customer makes a payment
2️⃣ Funds are first received by your Stripe Connect Account.
Funds are first received by your Stripe Connect Account.
3️⃣ After processing and verification, the funds are scheduled for payout.
After processing and verification
4️⃣ Stripe transfers the payout to your bank account according to your payout schedule.
Stripe transfers the payout to your bank account
payout schedule
📌 Important: Your customers’ payments are NOT instantly available in your bank account. They must first go through Stripe’s processing period and payout delay (explained below).
Important:
NOT instantly available
Stripe’s processing period and payout delay

## ⏳ 2. Payment Processing Time: SEPA vs. Credit Cards

⏳ 2. Payment Processing Time: SEPA vs. Credit Cards

## 💳 Credit Card Payments

💳 Credit Card Payments
✔ Processed instantly
Processed instantly
✔ Once the payment is processed, Stripe applies a payout delay for security and fraud prevention.
payout delay

## 🏦 SEPA Direct Debit Payments

🏦 SEPA Direct Debit Payments
⏳ Take at least 3 working days to be validated
Take at least 3 working days
🔄 Available for payout only after processing is complete, subject to the Payout Delay.
only after processing is complete
💡 Example:
Example:
- A SEPA payment made on Monday is completed by Thursday at the earliest.
A SEPA payment made on Monday is completed by Thursday at the earliest.
SEPA payment made on Monday
- A credit card payment made on Monday is processed instantly but available for payout only after the configured Payout Delay.
A credit card payment made on Monday is processed instantly but available for payout only after the configured Payout Delay.
credit card payment made on Monday
available for payout only after the configured Payout Delay

## 🚀 3. Understanding Payout Delay (T+3 Days Minimum)

🚀 3. Understanding Payout Delay (T+3 Days Minimum)
Stripe applies a payout delay for security and fraud prevention on all online transactions. This means that a payment via credit card is available at least 3 days later, the same for SEPA payments, on top of the time needed for validation (as explained above).
payout delay
at least 3 days later
✔ Minimum delay: 3 days (this cannot be reduced).
Minimum delay: 3 days
✔ This delay ensures the payment is legitimate before being released.
legitimate
✔ Even if you are on daily payouts, this delay still applies.
daily payouts
🚨 Even with daily payouts, you will not receive a same-day payout for transactions made that day!
Even with daily payouts, you will not receive a same-day payout for transactions made that day!

## 📅 4. Choosing Your Payout Frequency

📅 4. Choosing Your Payout Frequency
You can customize how often Stripe sends payouts to your bank:
customize
Payout Frequency
Payout Frequency
How It Works
How It Works
Best For
Best For
Daily Payouts
Daily Payouts
Funds that complete the payout delay are sent every day
High-volume businesses
Weekly Payouts
Weekly Payouts
Payouts are collected from a Thursday morning until the following Wednesday night. They are then sent every Friday; funds arrive Monday
every Friday
Monday
Most businesses
Monthly Payouts
Monthly Payouts
You choose a specific day each month
specific day
Long-term cash flow planning
💡 Weekly payouts are the most common because they balance cash flow and processing time.
Weekly payouts are the most common because they balance cash flow and processing time.

## 🔄 5. What Transactions Are Included in Your Payout?

🔄 5. What Transactions Are Included in Your Payout?
A payout only includes transactions that have cleared Stripe’s processing time.
only includes transactions that have cleared Stripe’s processing time

## 🗓️ Weekly Payouts - Example Breakdown

🗓️ Weekly Payouts - Example Breakdown
If your payout is scheduled for Monday, it will include:
scheduled for Monday
- Transactions made Thursday (last week) – Wednesday (this week)
Transactions made Thursday (last week) – Wednesday (this week)
Thursday (last week) – Wednesday (this week)
- Payout is sent Friday, arrives Monday
Payout is sent Friday, arrives Monday
sent Friday, arrives Monday
Transaction Date
Transaction Date
Included in Payout?
Included in Payout?
Thursday (last week)
✅ Yes
Monday (this week)
✅ Yes
Friday (this week)
❌ No (Will be in next week’s payout)

## 💰 6. Refunds & Disputes: How They Impact Payouts

💰 6. Refunds & Disputes: How They Impact Payouts
✔ Refunds are deducted from your next payout automatically.
Refunds are deducted from your next payout
✔ Disputes (chargebacks) are also deducted if lost.
Disputes (chargebacks) are also deducted
✔ If your balance becomes negative, Stripe will block future refunds until positive.
If your balance becomes negative
block future refunds

## 📌 Example: Refund Deduction

📌 Example: Refund Deduction
- Expected Payout: 1,000€
Expected Payout: 1,000€
Expected Payout: 1,000€
- Refund Issued: 300€
Refund Issued: 300€
Refund Issued: 300€
- Final Payout: 700€
Final Payout: 700€
Final Payout: 700€
🚨 If your balance is negative, Stripe may withhold future payouts or require additional funds to cover the deficit.
If your balance is negative, Stripe may withhold future payouts or require additional funds to cover the deficit.

what payments are included in a payout

### Frequently Asked Questions (FAQ)
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/12441759-new-vat-rules-for-eu-clients-starting-october-2025

From October 2025, Bsport will apply new VAT rules to some of our EU clients. This change is required by law and applies to all companies and associations using Bsport in the European Union (outside France) who do not have a valid intra-community VAT number registered in their back-office.
October 2025
who do not have a valid intra-community VAT number

## Why is this changing?

Why is this changing?
Bsport provides software-as-a-service (SaaS), which the European Union classifies as an “electronically supplied service” (our software platform). According to EU law, electronic services must be taxed differently depending on whether the client provides a valid intra-community VAT number (often called an “EU VAT number” or “VIES”).
EU VAT number
VIES
- If you have a valid intra-community VAT number (EU VAT) → no VAT is applied to your invoice. You are considered a taxable business. Bsport will issue your invoices without VAT, and you will declare the VAT yourself in your own country (this is called the “reverse-charge mechanism”).
If you have a valid intra-community VAT number (EU VAT) → no VAT is applied to your invoice. You are considered a taxable business. Bsport will issue your invoices without VAT, and you will declare the VAT yourself in your own country (this is called the “reverse-charge mechanism”).
intra-community VAT number (EU VAT)
- If you do not have a valid intra-community VAT number → You are considered a non-taxable person (like a consumer). In this case, Bsport is required to add VAT to your invoices at your country’s local rate (e.g. Germany 19%, Spain 21%, Italy 22%).​​
If you do not have a valid intra-community VAT number → You are considered a non-taxable person (like a consumer). In this case, Bsport is required to add VAT to your invoices at your country’s local rate (e.g. Germany 19%, Spain 21%, Italy 22%).​​
not
This is not a new rule: it comes directly from the EU VAT Directive (2006/112/EC, Article 58) and the VAT e-commerce package that entered into force on 1 July 2021.
EU VAT Directive (2006/112/EC, Article 58)
From October 2025, Bsport will fully apply these rules in all cases. This is not a decision made by Bsport, but a legal requirement for all software providers in the EU.
October 2025
legal requirement for all software providers in the EU
VAT vs. EU VAT: What’s the difference?
VAT vs. EU VAT: What’s the difference?
- VAT (Value Added Tax): A consumption tax applied in every EU country (e.g. Germany 19%, Spain 21%, Italy 22%).
VAT (Value Added Tax): A consumption tax applied in every EU country (e.g. Germany 19%, Spain 21%, Italy 22%).
VAT (Value Added Tax):
- EU VAT / Intra-community VAT number: A special tax identification number that exempts businesses (and some associations) from VAT when purchasing services across EU borders.​​
EU VAT / Intra-community VAT number: A special tax identification number that exempts businesses (and some associations) from VAT when purchasing services across EU borders.​​
EU VAT / Intra-community VAT number:
tax identification number
👉 Simply put: Having a VAT number from your national tax authority registered in the EU VIES system allows you to avoid VAT charges on Bsport invoices.
VAT number from your national tax authority registered in the EU VIES system
https://ec.europa.eu/taxation_customs/vies/#/vat-validation

## What does this mean for you?

What does this mean for you?
- EU companies without an EU VAT number → VAT will be charged at your local rate.
EU companies without an EU VAT number → VAT will be charged at your local rate.
EU companies without an EU VAT number
- EU companies with a valid EU VAT number → No VAT will be charged.
EU companies with a valid EU VAT number → No VAT will be charged.
EU companies with a valid EU VAT number
- EU Associations:​​If the association has an EU VAT number → No VAT will be charged.If the association does not have an EU VAT number → VAT must be charged, as the association is considered an “end consumer”.
EU Associations:​​
EU Associations:​​
- If the association has an EU VAT number → No VAT will be charged.
If the association has an EU VAT number → No VAT will be charged.
- If the association does not have an EU VAT number → VAT must be charged, as the association is considered an “end consumer”.
If the association does not have an EU VAT number → VAT must be charged, as the association is considered an “end consumer”.
not

## In which cases VAT will not be charged

In which cases VAT will not be charged
- EU clients without EU VAT: VAT will be charged at the local rate.
EU clients without EU VAT: VAT will be charged at the local rate.
EU clients without EU VAT
- Associations with no VAT number: VAT will be charged.
Associations with no VAT number: VAT will be charged.
Associations with no VAT number
- Non-EU clients (UK, Switzerland, USA, Canada, etc.): No VAT will be charged, as they are outside EU VAT rules.​​
Non-EU clients (UK, Switzerland, USA, Canada, etc.): No VAT will be charged, as they are outside EU VAT rules.​​
Non-EU clients

## Will this cost me more?

Will this cost me more?
- For businesses and VAT-registered associations: No. VAT is not a cost; it can be reclaimed via your local tax return.
For businesses and VAT-registered associations: No. VAT is not a cost; it can be reclaimed via your local tax return.
For businesses and VAT-registered associations
- For associations and entities not registered for VAT: Yes, VAT is a cost, since only VAT-registered entities can recover it.​​
For associations and entities not registered for VAT: Yes, VAT is a cost, since only VAT-registered entities can recover it.​​
For associations and entities not registered for VAT

back-office

Enter your valid intra-community VAT number.

## Key takeaways

Key takeaways
- From October 2025, EU clients without an intra-community VAT number will be charged VAT.
From October 2025, EU clients without an intra-community VAT number will be charged VAT.
October 2025
- Associations are treated the same as companies: no VAT only if they have an EU VAT number.
Associations are treated the same as companies: no VAT only if they have an EU VAT number.
Associations are treated the same as companies
- Non-EU clients will not be charged VAT.
Non-EU clients will not be charged VAT.
Non-EU clients
- Businesses and VAT-registered associations can reclaim VAT, so it’s not a real cost.
Businesses and VAT-registered associations can reclaim VAT, so it’s not a real cost.
reclaim VAT
- Associations or entities not VAT-registered will bear the VAT.
Associations or entities not VAT-registered will bear the VAT.
💡 If you’re unsure about your VAT status, please consult your accountant or local tax authority.​​
The Bsport Team
The Bsport Team

## Frequently Asked Questions (FAQ)

Frequently Asked Questions (FAQ)
1. What if I get my VAT number after October 2025?
1. What if I get my VAT number after October 2025?
If you enter a valid EU VAT number after October, VAT will stop being applied to future invoices. For past invoices, you can reclaim VAT through your local tax return with your accountant.
2. I already entered my VAT number, how do I know if it’s valid?
2. I already entered my VAT number, how do I know if it’s valid?
Your VAT number must be valid in the EU VIES system. If it’s invalid, VAT will still be charged. You can check validity here: VIES EU Validation Tool.
3. I am outside the EU, do I need to do anything?
3. I am outside the EU, do I need to do anything?
No. Clients outside the EU (e.g. UK, Switzerland, USA) are not charged VAT.
4. I am a French association with VAT exemption, do I pay VAT?
4. I am a French association with VAT exemption, do I pay VAT?
If you are exempt, you must send us an attestation de non-assujettissement. Without this, French VAT will apply.
5. Can I recover VAT charged on Bsport invoices?
5. Can I recover VAT charged on Bsport invoices?
Yes, if you are VAT-registered. Your accountant will reclaim it through your VAT return. Associations or non-VAT-registered entities cannot recover VAT.
6. How long does it take to get an EU VAT number?
6. How long does it take to get an EU VAT number?
The process depends on your country, but typically takes 3–8 weeks depending on the country. We recommend applying as soon as possible.

### What is VeriFactu?
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/13375112-how-to-activate-verifactu-on-your-platform

## What is VeriFactu?

VeriFactu is the electronic invoicing system introduced by the Spanish Tax Agency to ensure traceability, transparency, and authenticity in business transactions. Its main goal is to guarantee that all issued invoices are recorded and reported to the Tax Agency in real or near real time, helping to prevent tax fraud and simplify compliance for businesses.
In this guide, we’ll explain how to activate the integration with VeriFactu through Fiskaly, our fiscal partner that allows you to connect your invoicing system to the Spanish Tax Agency easily, automatically, and securely.
The integration has to be activated, please reach out to your Account Manager to activate it.
For now, TicketBai is not supported (Basque Country).

## Introduction

In order to be able to sign your invoices you must complete all the following steps and prerequisites.
Invoices created before completing theses steps won't be sent automatically. You'll have to do it manually.

## 1. Spanish Electronic Certificate

The first step of this integration is to obtain your Spanish Electronic Certification. To do so, please follow the steps indicated here 💡
Please note:
- Cost: 24€ (without taxes)
Cost: 24€ (without taxes)
24€
- Can be completed:Fully online (requires installing specific software), orIn person at an AEAT office: https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/
Can be completed:
- Fully online (requires installing specific software), or
Fully online (requires installing specific software), or
- In person at an AEAT office: https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/
In person at an AEAT office: https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/
AEAT
- Estimated timing:Online: a few hoursIn-person appointment: usually within ~1 week
Estimated timing:
- Online: a few hours
Online: a few hours
- In-person appointment: usually within ~1 week
In-person appointment: usually within ~1 week
~1 week
- ValidityYour certificate will be valid for 2 years
Validity
- Your certificate will be valid for 2 years
Your certificate will be valid for 2 years
2 years

Verifactu

can only be signed using the Spanish Electronic Certificate of the company representative

## 3. Invoice Eligibility for Verifactu

An invoice can be sent to Verifactu if and only if all of the following conditions are met:
if and only if
- The invoice is opened or a refund invoiceMeaning the invoice has a PDF For example, subscriptions have draft invoices (for future ones), these are not opened invoicesIt doesn't mean the invoice has been paid

opened or a refund
- Meaning the invoice has a PDF For example, subscriptions have draft invoices (for future ones), these are not opened invoices

It doesn't mean the invoice has been paid
- The invoice is not voided
The invoice is not voided
not voided
- The invoice does not come from a migration
The invoice does not come from a migration
not come from a migration
- The invoice contains at least one invoice item
The invoice contains at least one invoice item
at least one invoice item

The invoice is not a partial refund from a payment pack
not
If any of these conditions are not met, the invoice will not be sent to Verifactu.

## 4. Invoice Signature Flows

In order to send an invoice to taxe authorities we first make sure that the invoice has the right information. Invoices with wrong information will be rejected by tax authorities and it won't be possible to send them again. This is why we do a first check before sending the invoices.

won't be possible to send them again

Fail

You must complete the missing information

rejected by Verifactu

### Intercom
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/11426418-tse-integration-with-fiskaly

To comply with German law, all cash register systems must be connected to a TSE (technical security system). The goal is to prevent any manipulation of sales data after it has been recorded.
TSE (technical security system)
At bsport, we’ve integrated with fiskaly, the first company certified by the German Federal Cyber Security Authority with maximum validity for a cloud-based TSE solution. fiskaly handles the secure, tamper-proof signing of your transactions, keeping your studio compliant with KassenSichV.
fiskaly
This guide walks you through everything you need to know as a studio manager.

## 🔧 1. Activating fiskaly in the Backoffice

Activating fiskaly in the Backoffice
Enabling fiskaly is quick and easy:
- Log into your bsport Backoffice
Log into your bsport Backoffice
- Navigate to Settings > bsport subscription > Available Add-ons
Navigate to Settings > bsport subscription > Available Add-ons
Settings > bsport subscription > Available Add-ons
- Locate the fiskaly TSE integration upsell
Locate the fiskaly TSE integration upsell
fiskaly TSE integration
- Click See more, tick the confirmation checkbox, and click Confirm
Click See more, tick the confirmation checkbox, and click Confirm
See more,
Confirm
Your TSE environment will be automatically created. No technical setup is needed: you can start using your compliant register immediately.
💡 Note: The TSE integration is a paid add-on to cover the cost of our certified third-party provider, fiskaly.
paid add-on

## 🧾 2. What happens when you create an invoice?

What happens when you create an invoice?
When an invoice is generated on bsport:
- A transaction is initiated in the fiskaly system
A transaction is initiated in the fiskaly system
- There is no visible change at this stage
There is no visible change at this stage
💡 Think of it as starting the legal record, but not finalising it yet.

## 💳 3. What happens when the invoice is paid?

What happens when the invoice is paid?
When the customer pays:
- fiskaly signs the transaction: it can no longer be altered
fiskaly signs the transaction: it can no longer be altered
signs the transaction: it can no longer be altered
- A QR code appears on the PDF receipt
A QR code appears on the PDF receipt
QR code
- A chip displays on the invoice page in your Backoffice
A chip displays on the invoice page in your Backoffice
chip
This confirms that the record of the transaction is now tamper-proof.
💡 Your member's online purchases are also signed by fiskaly. In this case, invoice creation and payment occur instantly.

## ⚙️ 4. Where to find your legal information

Where to find your legal information
All relevant legal and fiscal information is available directly in your Backoffice:
- Go to Settings > Billing
Go to Settings > Billing
Settings > Billing
- Scroll to Kassensicherungsverordnung (Germany)
Scroll to Kassensicherungsverordnung (Germany)
Kassensicherungsverordnung (Germany)
Here, you'll find:
- An overview of the fiskaly integration
An overview of the fiskaly integration
- TSE version and manufacturer details
TSE version and manufacturer details
- A link to fiskaly's certification documents
A link to fiskaly's certification documents
fiskaly's certification documents
- A link to bsport’s Procedural Documentation
A link to bsport’s Procedural Documentation
bsport’s Procedural Documentation

## 🕵️ 5. In case of an audit

In case of an audit
If you're audited by German tax authorities:
- Contact your Account Manager to export your fiskaly-signed data
Contact your Account Manager to export your fiskaly-signed data
- Provide your auditor with bsport’s Procedural Documentation
Provide your auditor with bsport’s Procedural Documentation
bsport’s Procedural Documentation
🔐 All transactions are securely stored in fiskaly’s system and can be retrieved at any time.

## 📌 Frequently Asked Questions

Do I need a TSE?→ Yes, if you use the bsport Backoffice to process on-site payments.
Do I need a TSE?
→ Only a tax advisor can confirm if exceptions apply to your case.
Will previous transactions be signed by fiskaly?→ No. Only transactions created and paid after the TSE was activated are signed.
Will previous transactions be signed by fiskaly?
created and paid

## 🤝 Need Help?

Our support team is here for you. Reach out via the in-app chat or at support@bsport.io

### Intercom
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/13529640-how-to-set-up-sequential-invoice-numbering

In some countries, invoices must follow a sequential numbering system to ensure proper accounting and prevent fraud.
If you’re already using bsport and haven’t set this up yet, this article explains how and when to activate sequential invoice numbering for your studio.
how and when to activate sequential invoice numbering
👉 If you already filled in your invoice numbering during onboarding, you don’t need to do anything.
If you already filled in your invoice numbering during onboarding, you don’t need to do anything.

## What is sequential invoice numbering?

Sequential invoice numbering means that each new invoice gets a number that follows the previous one, without gaps or duplicates.
On bsport, an invoice number can include:
- A prefix (optional)
A prefix (optional)
prefix
- A date (year, or year + month — mandatory)
A date (year, or year + month — mandatory)
date
- A sequential number
A sequential number
sequential number
- A suffix (optional)
A suffix (optional)
suffix
Example:​INV-2026-000004-FR
⚠️ Important note: Once this numbering is set up, it cannot be edited.
Important note:
it cannot be edited

## Who needs to set this up?

You need to take action if:
- Your studio was created before this feature existed, and
Your studio was created before this feature existed, and
before this feature existed
- You haven’t defined your invoice numbering yet.
You haven’t defined your invoice numbering yet.
If your studio is new and you already completed this step during onboarding, you’re all set ✅
If you already asked for sequential numbering to be done by bsport in the past, you will not be able to change the numbering from the back office.

## Where to set up invoice numbering

- Go to Settings > Billing in your bsport back office
Go to Settings > Billing in your bsport back office
Settings > Billing
- Go to the Invoice numbering section
Go to the Invoice numbering section
Invoice numbering
- Define your numbering format (prefix, date, suffix)
Define your numbering format (prefix, date, suffix)
Once confirmed, this configuration is locked and will apply automatically based on the start date you choose.
configuration is locked

## Choose when sequential numbering should start

You’ll be asked to select when sequential numbering should apply.
when
⚠️ Important note: Once this numbering is set up, it cannot be edited.
Important note:
it cannot be edited

## Option 1: Beginning of next month (recommended)

This option is ideal if you don’t use external accounting software.
don’t use external accounting software
- All invoices created before next month keep their current ID
All invoices created before next month keep their current ID
before next month
- Invoices created from next month onward will use sequential numbering
Invoices created from next month onward will use sequential numbering
from next month onward
- The numbering will start at 1
The numbering will start at 1

## Option 2: Beginning of current month

This option is mainly for studios that already use external accounting software.
already use external accounting software
- Sequential numbering is applied retroactively to invoices created since the start of the current month
Sequential numbering is applied retroactively to invoices created since the start of the current month
- In the interface, existing invoices will display the new sequential number
In the interface, existing invoices will display the new sequential number
- Invoice PDFs created in the past will still show their original ID (PDFs cannot be edited)
Invoice PDFs created in the past will still show their original ID (PDFs cannot be edited)
Invoice PDFs created in the past will still show their original ID
All new invoices will have the correct sequential number both in the interface and on the PDF.

## Using an external accounting software?

If you recreate bsport invoices in another accounting tool, you can make sure both systems stay aligned.
- First select "Beginning of current month" as start date
First select "Beginning of current month" as start date
- Then, enable the option “I use an external accounting provider”
Then, enable the option “I use an external accounting provider”
“I use an external accounting provider”
- Choose the starting number for bsport invoices
Choose the starting number for bsport invoices
starting number
We recommend:
- Checking the last invoice number used in your external software
Checking the last invoice number used in your external software
last invoice number
- Adding +1
Adding +1
- Using that number as the starting point on bsport
Using that number as the starting point on bsport
This ensures continuity between both systems.

## ⚠️ Important to know ⚠️

- Once invoice numbering is set, it cannot be changed
Once invoice numbering is set, it cannot be changed
it cannot be changed
- Invoices created before activation keep their original ID
Invoices created before activation keep their original ID
- Sequential numbering applies only to invoices created on bsport
Sequential numbering applies only to invoices created on bsport

### Prerequisites
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/14138282-understand-invoice-payment-statuses

## Prerequisites

- Access to the invoice details page in Billing > Invoices
Access to the invoice details page in Billing > Invoices
- An existing invoice
An existing invoice

## Detailed explanation

On the invoice details page, several payment amounts are displayed. Each one reflects a different stage of the payment process.

## 1. Total due

- This is the total amount of the invoice.
This is the total amount of the invoice.
- It corresponds to the full price that must be paid.​
It corresponds to the full price that must be paid.​

## 2. Payment received

- This is the total amount already paid successfully.
This is the total amount already paid successfully.
- These payments are confirmed and cannot fail.​
These payments are confirmed and cannot fail.​

## 3. Payment planned

- This is the total of future scheduled payments.
This is the total of future scheduled payments.
- These payments have not yet been attempted (e.g. instalments).​
These payments have not yet been attempted (e.g. instalments).​

## 4. Payment pending

This includes payments currently being processed.For example:
- SEPA direct debit
SEPA direct debit
- BACS payments
BACS payments
⚠️ These payments can still fail.

## 5. Payment outstanding

This is the remaining amount to be paid.
It is calculated as: Total due – (Payment received + Payment planned + Payment pending)
Total due – (Payment received + Payment planned + Payment pending)
👉 If this amount is greater than 0, the invoice is not fully covered.

## Example

For a 100€ invoice:
- Initially:No payments → 100€ outstanding
Initially:
- No payments → 100€ outstanding
No payments → 100€ outstanding
100€ outstanding
- After setting up 10 instalments of 10€:100€ planned → 0€ outstanding
After setting up 10 instalments of 10€:
- 100€ planned → 0€ outstanding
100€ planned → 0€ outstanding
0€ outstanding
- If one payment is processing:80€ planned + 20€ pending → still 0€ outstanding
If one payment is processing:
- 80€ planned + 20€ pending → still 0€ outstanding
80€ planned + 20€ pending → still 0€ outstanding
0€ outstanding
- If one payment succeeds:20€ received + 80€ planned → still 0€ outstanding
If one payment succeeds:
- 20€ received + 80€ planned → still 0€ outstanding
20€ received + 80€ planned → still 0€ outstanding
0€ outstanding
- If one payment fails:20€ received + 70€ planned → 10€ outstanding
If one payment fails:
- 20€ received + 70€ planned → 10€ outstanding
20€ received + 70€ planned → 10€ outstanding
10€ outstanding

## Additional information

⚠️ ImportantIf Payment outstanding > 0, the invoice is overdue. An action is required (manual payment or retry).
Important
Payment outstanding > 0
💡 Good to knowA failed planned payment creates the same situation as a canceled one, with the failed attempt visible in the payment list.
Good to know

## What’s inside the export

The ZIP file is named in the format invoices_YYYY-MM_[id].zip. It contains:

## 📄 One PDF per invoice

- Each PDF is named: YYYY-MM-DD_[invoice-number]_[type].pdfExamples:2025-03-15_INV-000123_invoice.pdf2025-03-20_CN-000456_credit-note.pdf
Each PDF is named: YYYY-MM-DD_[invoice-number]_[type].pdf

- 2025-03-15_INV-000123_invoice.pdf

- 2025-03-20_CN-000456_credit-note.pdf
2025-03-20_CN-000456_credit-note.pdf
- The date in the filename is the invoice issue date, displayed in your studio's local timezone.
The date in the filename is the invoice issue date, displayed in your studio's local timezone.
your studio's local timezone

## 📊 A CSV file

Named invoices_manifest.csv, this spreadsheet lists every invoice in the export. It includes:
Column
Description
invoice_number
Invoice or credit note number
issue_date
Date and time the invoice was issued (in your timezone)
customer_name
Client's full name
amount_due
Amount (in your currency, e.g. EUR)
currency
Currency code
invoice_type
"Invoice" or "Credit Note"
pdf_filename
The name of the matching PDF in the ZIP
invoice_internal_identifier
Internal unique reference
company_id
Your studio's identifier
This file can be opened in Excel, Google Sheets, or imported into accounting software.

## Additional information

## 📁 Documents downloaded

- The export covers regular invoices and credit notes only. Internal adjustments and migration records are excluded.
The export covers regular invoices and credit notes only. Internal adjustments and migration records are excluded.
regular invoices and credit notes

## ⚠️ Limitations

- Only invoices from March 2025 onwards are available
Only invoices from March 2025 onwards are available
March 2025 onwards
- You cannot export the current or future month
You cannot export the current or future month
current or future month
- Migrated invoices are excluded
Migrated invoices are excluded
Migrated invoices are excluded
- Invoices without PDF attached will be missing from ZIP. They are still listed in the CSV manifest.
Invoices without PDF attached will be missing from ZIP. They are still listed in the CSV manifest.

## 💡 Good to know

- Exports are ready on the 1st day of the following monthFor example, the March 2025 export is automatically prepared on April 1st.If unavailable on the 1st, try again within 24 hours. ZIP generation may be pending.
Exports are ready on the 1st day of the following month
1st day of the following month
- For example, the March 2025 export is automatically prepared on April 1st.
For example, the March 2025 export is automatically prepared on April 1st.
- If unavailable on the 1st, try again within 24 hours. ZIP generation may be pending.
If unavailable on the 1st, try again within 24 hours. ZIP generation may be pending.
24 hours.
- You can download the same export multiple times
You can download the same export multiple times
- Each export covers one month only
Each export covers one month only
one month only
- The export reflects your studio's configured timezone. Issue dates and the invoices included are determined by when the month started and ended in your local time, not UTC.
The export reflects your studio's configured timezone. Issue dates and the invoices included are determined by when the month started and ended in your local time, not UTC.
configured timezone

## Common error messages

Message
What it means
What to do

The selected month is before the feature launch date.
Select March 2025 or later.

You selected the current or a future month.
Wait until the month is fully over.

Try again later the same day or the next day.

Contact the support team.

Wait a few hours and try again.

Your studio had no invoices before this period — the feature only covers months where invoices exist.
No action needed.

Try again; if it persists, contact support.

## Frequently asked questions

- Yes. The file is already prepared — each download simply re-opens the same ZIP. There is no limit on downloads.
Yes. The file is already prepared — each download simply re-opens the same ZIP. There is no limit on downloads.

Can I download exports for multiple months at once?

- No. Each export covers exactly one calendar month. You need to repeat the process for each month you want.

Yes. Both regular invoices and credit notes (refunds) are included and clearly labelled in the PDF filename and CSV.
- My export says "No invoices for this period" — is something wrong? No. It means your studio simply had no billing activity during that month. This is normal for periods before you started using the platform's invoicing, or quiet months with no transactions.

### How to Manage Payments, Unpaid Balances, and Payment Methods Effectively
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/14532803-how-to-manage-payments-unpaid-balances-and-payment-methods-effectively

## How to Manage Payments, Unpaid Balances, and Payment Methods Effectively

Managing payments, unpaid balances, and customer payment methods is essential for ensuring smooth financial operations. This guide provides an overview of key processes and actionable steps to handle common payment-related scenarios.

## Overview of Payment Management

Payment management involves tracking unpaid balances, processing invoices, and managing customer payment methods. By understanding these processes, you can ensure timely payments and maintain accurate financial records.

## Handling Unpaid Balances and Invoices

### Collecting Payments for Unpaid Balances

If no payment data is entered for a customer account, the internal account balance will show as negative.
This situation typically arises when payment was not successfully collected or recorded, indicating that the client has not paid or the payment has not been processed.
To see a list of members with unpaid balances: ​Option 1:
To see a list of members with unpaid balances:
Option 1:
- Go to Marketing > Smartlists > Create a smartlist.
Go to Marketing > Smartlists > Create a smartlist.
- Create a new smartlist​
Create a new smartlist​
- Select Add Parameters > Member information > Account balance.​
Select Add Parameters > Member information > Account balance.​
- Select the option less than or equal to -1 and then add​
Select the option less than or equal to -1 and then add​
less than or equal to -1
add
- You can then see at the bottom of the page under Overview a list of members who are in a minus account balance and/or have an unpaid invoice. ​
You can then see at the bottom of the page under Overview a list of members who are in a minus account balance and/or have an unpaid invoice. ​
Option 2: This is just to see unpaid invoices and not members who are in a minus balance.
Option 2:
- Go to Analytics > Reports​
Go to Analytics > Reports​
- Select Unpaid Invoices
Select Unpaid Invoices
3. Generate the report. ​
You will then has a list of all unpaid invoices.

## Managing Payment Methods and Charging Unpaid Invoices

- You can add a payment method to a member's profile if they do not currently have one. To do this, go to the member profile and scroll down on the left side and you will see the option to add a payment method.​​
You can add a payment method to a member's profile if they do not currently have one. To do this, go to the member profile and scroll down on the left side and you will see the option to add a payment method.​
- If there is already a saved payment method and the member is in a minus account balance, this could be due to an unpaid subscription. First you can charge the account balance with the payment method now on file by clicking Adjust Balance.​Then click Next.​You can then charge the payment method that is on file. If the minus balance is due to an unpaid subscription, make sure to go to Billing on the member profile and select the subscription at the bottom.​On their subscription on the right side you will have the option to "add" the payment method. This will in turn give you the option to select the payment method that the member now has on file.​
If there is already a saved payment method and the member is in a minus account balance, this could be due to an unpaid subscription. First you can charge the account balance with the payment method now on file by clicking Adjust Balance.​
Then click Next.​
You can then charge the payment method that is on file. If the minus balance is due to an unpaid subscription, make sure to go to Billing on the member profile and select the subscription at the bottom.​
On their subscription on the right side you will have the option to "add" the payment method. This will in turn give you the option to select the payment method that the member now has on file.​
- If the member has an unpaid invoice, you can go to their profile > click on take payment under the field Unpaid Invoices.​This will give you the option to charge the member through the =ir saved payment method. ​
If the member has an unpaid invoice, you can go to their profile > click on take payment under the field Unpaid Invoices.​
This will give you the option to charge the member through the =ir saved payment method. ​

## Resolving Common Payment Issues

- Always ensure that payment methods are up-to-date to avoid delays in processing payments.
Always ensure that payment methods are up-to-date to avoid delays in processing payments.
- Regularly monitor unpaid balances and invoices to maintain accurate financial records.
Regularly monitor unpaid balances and invoices to maintain accurate financial records.
- Use automated tools and reports to track and manage outstanding payments efficiently.
Use automated tools and reports to track and manage outstanding payments efficiently.
By following these guidelines, you can effectively manage payments, unpaid balances, and customer payment methods, ensuring a seamless experience for both your business and your customers.

### How to Manage and Disable SEPA Payments in bsport
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/14535515-how-to-manage-and-disable-sepa-payments-in-bsport

## How to Manage and Disable SEPA Payments in bsport

SEPA (Single Euro Payments Area) payments are a common method for processing transactions in bsport. This guide explains how to manage and disable SEPA payments, whether for new transactions or existing subscriptions, and clarifies the implications of disabling SEPA.

## Overview of SEPA Payments

SEPA payments allow for efficient and standardised euro transactions across participating countries. In bsport, SEPA can be used for both one-time payments and recurring subscriptions. However, there may be scenarios where you need to disable SEPA as a payment method or stop automatic SEPA payments for specific subscriptions.

## Disabling SEPA as a Payment Method

To prevent SEPA from being used for future transactions, such as trial sessions or new subscriptions, follow these steps:
- Navigate to Settings > Payment Methods in the Bsport dashboard.
Navigate to Settings > Payment Methods in the Bsport dashboard.
Settings > Payment Methods
- Locate the SEPA payment option.
Locate the SEPA payment option.
- Turn off or uncheck the SEPA payment method to disable it for future use.​
Turn off or uncheck the SEPA payment method to disable it for future use.​
This action ensures that SEPA will no longer be available as a payment option for members making new purchases via the Member portal or app. However, staff can still process SEPA payments manually from the Back Office if needed.

## Managing SEPA for Existing Subscriptions

If you need to stop automatic SEPA direct debit payments for an existing subscription, follow these steps:
- Open the bsport dashboard and go to Transactions > Direct Debits.
Open the bsport dashboard and go to Transactions > Direct Debits.
Transactions > Direct Debits
- In the list of active subscriptions, locate the relevant invoice and click the pencil icon to edit it.
In the list of active subscriptions, locate the relevant invoice and click the pencil icon to edit it.
- Select View Invoice to access the payment details.
Select View Invoice to access the payment details.
View Invoice
- In the Payment Scheduled section, click the three dots next to the customer’s instalment.
In the Payment Scheduled section, click the three dots next to the customer’s instalment.
Payment Scheduled
- Choose Deactivate to cancel the automatic SEPA payment.​​Once unscheduled, the invoice will need to be collected manually. This process ensures that no further automatic SEPA payments are processed for the subscription.
Choose Deactivate to cancel the automatic SEPA payment.​
Deactivate
Once unscheduled, the invoice will need to be collected manually. This process ensures that no further automatic SEPA payments are processed for the subscription.

## Implications of Disabling SEPA

Disabling SEPA as a payment method has the following implications:
- New Transactions: Members will no longer see SEPA as an available payment option for online purchases via the Member portal or app.
New Transactions: Members will no longer see SEPA as an available payment option for online purchases via the Member portal or app.
New Transactions:
- Existing Mandates: Disabling SEPA does not automatically cancel existing SEPA payments or mandates. These will continue unless manually updated.
Existing Mandates: Disabling SEPA does not automatically cancel existing SEPA payments or mandates. These will continue unless manually updated.
Existing Mandates:
- Back Office Transactions: Staff can still process SEPA payments manually from the Back Office, even if SEPA is disabled for members.
Back Office Transactions: Staff can still process SEPA payments manually from the Back Office, even if SEPA is disabled for members.
Back Office Transactions:
- Customer Payment Methods: If you want to prevent future SEPA transactions for specific customers, you must manually update their payment methods.
Customer Payment Methods: If you want to prevent future SEPA transactions for specific customers, you must manually update their payment methods.
Customer Payment Methods:
By understanding and managing SEPA payments effectively, you can ensure smooth payment operations while addressing specific business needs.

### AI assistant
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/11516652-coachmail-your-guide-to-building-more-advanced-emails

Once you have mastered the first steps of creating an email, as explained in this article, you can move on to using the more advanced features of the bsport email builder.
The builder now includes a set of powerful tools designed to help you create high-performing, visually engaging, and time-efficient marketing emails.
This guide will walk you through each of these features, with concrete examples and use cases to help you get the most out of the builder.
Below is what the updated interface looks like:
Blocks that include AI capabilities are marked with this green icon:
In this document we will cover the following features and how to use them efficiently:
- AI assistant (text, buttons, headers)
AI assistant (text, buttons, headers)
- Countdown timer
Countdown timer
- Company-saved content blocks
Company-saved content blocks
- Image gallery
Image gallery
- Saved uploads
Saved uploads

## AI assistant

## AI for text

### What is AI for text?

This feature leverages AI to assist with text creation and editing inside your email. Whether you need to correct grammar, rephrase for a different tone, expand ideas, or shorten your message, the Smart Text assistant can help.

### How to use it?

- Write your draft or ideas directly in the text box.
Write your draft or ideas directly in the text box.
- Click on the AI assistant to choose what you want help with: correcting tone, making it shorter, adding more detail, etc.
Click on the AI assistant to choose what you want help with: correcting tone, making it shorter, adding more detail, etc.

### Use cases & benefits?

- Create professional, polished messages faster.
Create professional, polished messages faster.
- Adapt tone based on your audience: friendly, formal.
Adapt tone based on your audience: friendly, formal.
- Example: You want to write a short promotion for a new class. Instead of writing it from scratch, write: "Promote our new yoga class", and ask the AI to expand text or rephrase it. Then change the tone to formal.
Example: You want to write a short promotion for a new class. Instead of writing it from scratch, write: "Promote our new yoga class", and ask the AI to expand text or rephrase it. Then change the tone to formal.

## AI for headers & buttons

### What is AI for headers & buttons?

This tool suggests high-impact headlines and button CTAs (calls to action), optimized for clarity and click-through rate.

### How to use it?

How to use it?
- Input the goal of your email or the email section (e.g., “Promote Pilates class” or “Drive booking for workshop”), and the AI will provide options you can insert directly.
Input the goal of your email or the email section (e.g., “Promote Pilates class” or “Drive booking for workshop”), and the AI will provide options you can insert directly.

### Use cases & benefits

Use cases & benefits
- Improve subject lines and CTAs to drive more engagement.
Improve subject lines and CTAs to drive more engagement.
- Example: Instead of writing “Join us now,” let the AI suggest “Book your spot – only 5 left!” or “Start your Pilates journey today.”
Example: Instead of writing “Join us now,” let the AI suggest “Book your spot – only 5 left!” or “Start your Pilates journey today.”

## AI for images

### What is AI for images?

This feature allows you to generate AI images directly inside the builder by writing a descriptive prompt and selecting a style.

### How to use it?

How to use it?
- Type a prompt (e.g., “sunrise yoga on a beach”) and pick a style like “realistic,” “flat illustration,” or “vintage.”
Type a prompt (e.g., “sunrise yoga on a beach”) and pick a style like “realistic,” “flat illustration,” or “vintage.”
- Upload the generated image into your email.
Upload the generated image into your email.

### Use cases & benefits

Use cases & benefits
- Easily create visuals that match the theme of your campaign.
Easily create visuals that match the theme of your campaign.
- Example: Launching a summer promo? Use a prompt like “happy group doing fitness outdoors summer sun” to instantly generate on-brand visuals.
Example: Launching a summer promo? Use a prompt like “happy group doing fitness outdoors summer sun” to instantly generate on-brand visuals.
Tip: Whenever possible, use authentic photos of your studio or clients for better trust and relevance.

## Countdown Timer

## What is the countdown timer?

A live countdown timer that visually displays the time left before a specific deadline.

## How to use it?

How to use it?
- Set a specific end date/time in the timer block.
Set a specific end date/time in the timer block.
- Add it to your email wherever urgency is needed.
Add it to your email wherever urgency is needed.

## Use Cases & Benefits

Use Cases & Benefits
- Create urgency for limited-time offers, flash sales, or special events.
Create urgency for limited-time offers, flash sales, or special events.
- Example: Add a timer to a campaign titled “Last 48 hours to get 20% off your first class.”
Example: Add a timer to a campaign titled “Last 48 hours to get 20% off your first class.”

## Company-saved blocks

## What are company-saved blocks?

This feature lets you save customised content blocks (like headers, footers, callouts) or a mix of all, to reuse across multiple emails.
These saved blocks will be saved at studio level.

## How to use it?

How to use it?
- Design a block you use often (e.g., your studio’s footer with address and social links, or a presentation block of your trainers), and click “Save”
Design a block you use often (e.g., your studio’s footer with address and social links, or a presentation block of your trainers), and click “Save”
- Next time, drag and drop it from your saved list.
Next time, drag and drop it from your saved list.

## Use Cases & Benefits

Use Cases & Benefits
- Save time by not recreating layouts from scratch.
Save time by not recreating layouts from scratch.
- Maintain brand consistency across all communications.
Maintain brand consistency across all communications.
- Example: Create a custom testimonial block once and reuse it in every monthly newsletter.
Example: Create a custom testimonial block once and reuse it in every monthly newsletter.

## Image gallery

## What is the image gallery?

A built-in gallery of high-quality, license-free images adapted to email formats.

## How to use it?

How to use it?
- Click on “Images” and search by keyword (e.g., “meditation,” “fitness,” “crossfit”).
Click on “Images” and search by keyword (e.g., “meditation,” “fitness,” “crossfit”).
- Choose and insert any image directly into your email without downloading/uploading.
Choose and insert any image directly into your email without downloading/uploading.

## Use Cases & Benefits

Use Cases & Benefits
- Quickly enrich your emails with nice-looking images when you don’t have custom visuals on hand.
Quickly enrich your emails with nice-looking images when you don’t have custom visuals on hand.
- Example: Promoting an event about mindfulness? Search for “calm nature” and choose a background image in seconds.
Example: Promoting an event about mindfulness? Search for “calm nature” and choose a background image in seconds.

## Saved uploads

## What is it?

What is it?
All images you upload into Unlayer are saved in your personal media library (directly in Unlayer).

## How to use it?

How to use it?
- Simply upload images as needed, they’ll automatically be stored.
Simply upload images as needed, they’ll automatically be stored.
- Use search to find and reuse past uploads easily.
Use search to find and reuse past uploads easily.

## Use Cases & Benefits

Use Cases & Benefits
- Build a personal studio asset library over time.
Build a personal studio asset library over time.
- Avoid repeated uploads of logos, banners, or instructor photos.
Avoid repeated uploads of logos, banners, or instructor photos.
- Example: Upload your logo once, and reuse it every time without hunting for the file again.
Example: Upload your logo once, and reuse it every time without hunting for the file again.

- Button: You add a button and add the variable and button parameters. On the right-hand side of your screen you'll find the button parameters. Under Action > URL, add the variable to activate the button and link it to the action.​
Button: You add a button and add the variable and button parameters. On the right-hand side of your screen you'll find the button parameters. Under Action > URL, add the variable to activate the button and link it to the action.​

### Intercom
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/4108119-unsubscribing-a-customer-to-the-newsletter

There are two ways to unsubscribe a customer to a Newsletter.
1st Method: Put an unsubscribe link directly in the email to be sent.
1st Method:
1st Step: Add an unsubscribe link in the email.
1st Step:
Add an "Unsubscribe" button.
In Marketing > Templates > Add a new template> Add a "Button" block > Click on the button > In the right part, add in "URL" the variable: {unsubscribe_link}.
2nd Step: Unsubscribe from the customer.
2nd Step:
After the customer receives the email, the customer can click on the button or the text and he will be redirected to the next page :
By clicking on "Unsubscribe", the client will withdraw from the subscription satisfactorily:
2nd Method: Unsubscribe the customer manually.
2nd Method:
Search for the customer in the search bar > On the customer's form, click on "Edit" > Uncheck the boxes "Accept notifications by email" and "Accept notifications by SMS" > Click on "Save".

## Inbox (3 articles)

### Intercom
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/8172973-communicate-with-your-members-with-the-new-messaging-interface

Elevate your communication with members through our cutting-edge messaging interface, seamlessly integrating multiple communication methods.
This interface is available to communicate with your session participants, reaching out to those who have booked, cancelled, or are eagerly awaiting a spot.
With the inbox add-on, it becomes possible to use this interface for 2-way communication with individual members, smartlists, or sessions. The inbox feature also offers additional options, which are described in this article.

## Step 1: Open the communication interface

Step 1: Open the communication interface
From a session page, the Communication button opens a Communication tab:
From the inbox, you have access to all conversations from members, smartlists and sessions:

### Step 2: Select your communication method

Step 2: Select your communication method
You can choose to send an email, an SMS or a push notification.
Note: SMS and push notifications are add-ons. To request your them, you can follow this guide on add-on integration.

### Step 3: Write your message

Step 3: Write your message
For push notifications and emails, your message needs a subject.

### Step 3 bis: Alternatively, select an email template

Step 3 bis: Alternatively, select an email template
If email is the chosen communication method, you can select an existing email template rather than writing a new mail.
Use the template selector pop-up to select the desired template.
Once the template is selected, 3 buttons are available:
- Eye button to see a full preview of the email
Eye button to see a full preview of the email
- Pencil button to edit the template (you will be redirected to the template editor)
Pencil button to edit the template (you will be redirected to the template editor)
- Cross button to cancel your choice
Cross button to cancel your choice
Note that the subject of the mail can be edited without modifying the template itself.

### Step 4: Use variables to enhance your message

Step 4: Use variables to enhance your message
You can use variables in all 3 communication methods. To make sure they are correctly formatted, a variable selector is available.
Selecting the desired variable will place it in the subject or the body of the message, depending on where your writing cursor is placed.
Please note that variables in email templates will also work, but not all variables: if you select, for example, a template using variables such as "gift card value" or "appointment date", such variables cannot work since there is no gift card or appointment to reference.

### Step 5: Send the message

Step 5: Send the message
For a Member [Inbox only]:
For a Member [Inbox only]:
- If the user does not have a registered email address or phone number, the Send button will be deactivated
If the user does not have a registered email address or phone number, the Send button will be deactivated
- If the member did not agree to receive marketing communication by SMS and/or by email, a warning will be displayed. You will still be able to send the communication, it's up to you to make sure the message is not a promotional message.
If the member did not agree to receive marketing communication by SMS and/or by email, a warning will be displayed. You will still be able to send the communication, it's up to you to make sure the message is not a promotional message.
For a Smartlist [Inbox only]:
For a Smartlist [Inbox only]:
The number of recipients will be displayed next to the Send button. By default, all recipients with a valid email (respectively, phone number) will be selected.
Clicking on the recipients will open a pop-up to display each recipient.
You can manually unselect some recipients.
Recipients without valid email or phone numbers will also appear in the list, but cannot be selected. Instead, a pencil button redirects you to their member profile edit form in a new tab to add the relevant info.
For a Session [From the session page or from the inbox]:
For a Session [From the session page or from the inbox]:
By default, no recipients are selected.
Clicking "Choose recipients" will open a pop-up where you can choose recipients from the bookings, the waitlist, or the cancellations. You can select several of those categories at once, and just like smartlist, each recipient can be individually unselected.
As for smartlists, recipients without valid email or phone numbers will appear, but cannot be selected.

### Step 6: See previous messages

Step 6: See previous messages
In this thread, you can see all previous messages in the conversation, and the date and time they were sent.
The type of message is distinguishable with an icon: Email, SMS or Push Notification.
For smartlists and sessions, you can see the number of recipients. Clicking on it or on the information icon will open the list of recipients, as well as the status of each message (sent/failed).
For each sent message, you can see how it was sent:
- For members, the inbox only displays direct messages
For members, the inbox only displays direct messages

For smartlists, the message can be manual or automatic

### General structure
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/8173987-manage-your-messages-with-the-inbox

The inbox feature is an add-on. To request the activation of the inbox, you can follow this guide on add-on integration.
The inbox feature is an add-on. To request the activation of the inbox, you can follow this guide on add-on integration.
This article details the functionalities of the inbox feature, which regroups all conversations with members, smartlists and sessions in one place. It assumes you are already familiar with the new messaging interface, whose functionalities are detailed in this article.
To make full use of the inbox, we recommend that you activate the setting to receive answers by email on the platform.
Please note that communication using SMS is an add-on. If you're in the US, UK or Canada, you can also receive answers from your members with 2-way SMS. To activate it, follow our guide on add-on integration.

## General structure

In the inbox, each conversation with a member, session or smartlist is called a thread. The inbox is split into 3 parts:
- The thread list section displays a list of threads ordered by the time since their last message. In this section, you can select a category of threads, filter by status, search for a specific thread, start a new thread, and change the status of each thread.
The thread list section displays a list of threads ordered by the time since their last message. In this section, you can select a category of threads, filter by status, search for a specific thread, start a new thread, and change the status of each thread.
- The chat section displays the history of messages for the selected thread. It is also in this section that you write and send new messages.
The chat section displays the history of messages for the selected thread. It is also in this section that you write and send new messages.
- The details section, collapsible, displays all the relevant information about the member, smartlist or session you're messaging.
The details section, collapsible, displays all the relevant information about the member, smartlist or session you're messaging.

## Start a new thread

To start a new thread from the inbox, click on the pencil button. Its behaviour will depend on which category of threads is selected: members, smartlists or sessions.

### Start a thread with a member

A pop-up will display a search bar. Use it to select the member you want to communicate with: you can look up members by their first name, last name, email or phone number.

### Start a thread with a smartlist

A pop-up will display a selector with a list of existing smartlists. There's also an option to create a new smartlist.

### Start a thread with a session

You are redirected to a calendar view. Navigate this calendar to select the desired session.

## Actions on threads

You can change the status of a thread by clicking on the "..." button on a thread item. 4 options are available:
- Mark as read
Mark as read
- Add to favourites
Add to favourites
- Mute
Mute
- Archive
Archive

### Mark a thread as unread

This option is only available for threads that have been read. It is useful in case you want to remind yourself of reading this thread later.
When a thread is marked as unread, the last message is treated as unread and therefore the thread item displays a red chip with 1 as the number of unread messages.

### Add a thread to Favourites

A Favourite thread will have a ⭐ icon on the right of its thread item. This icon will also appear in the Chat header after the contact name.
A favourite thread will always appear at the top of the list. If there are several favourites to display, first the favourites are sorted by time since the last message, and then the non-favourites are displayed, sorted by time as well.
You can filter the threads to only display Favourite threads, see below.
You can remove the thread from your favourites by clicking on the "..." button on the thread item and selecting the "Remove as favourite" option.

### Mute a thread

A Muted thread will have a 🔕 icon displayed on its thread icon and in the Chat header after the contact name.
When new messages are received in a muted thread, the number of unread messages on the thread item will be displayed in a grey circle rather than a red one. More importantly, newly received messages will not update the date of the last message for this thread, therefore it will not be pushed to the top of the list. Muted threads will gradually be moved to the end of the list of threads as other active threads will move to the top. However, if you read or send messages on a muted thread, the date of the last message will update.
You can filter the threads to only display Muted threads, see below.
You can unmute the thread by clicking on the "..." button on the thread item and selecting the "Unmute" option.

### Archive a thread

An Archived thread will disappear from the Thread tab, even when searching for it with the search tab.
However, the chat messages are not deleted. You can filter the threads to only display "Archived" threads, see below.
There are several ways of restoring an archived chat:
- by filtering for Archived threads to access the thread, and then clicking on the "..." button on the thread item and selecting the "Restore" option
by filtering for Archived threads to access the thread, and then clicking on the "..." button on the thread item and selecting the "Restore" option
- by receiving or sending a new message in the thread
by receiving or sending a new message in the thread

### Intercom
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/5790088-archive-a-member

If your member base comes from a migration from your previous provider, you may have many inactive members. It is also possible that some of your members have not been coming to your studio for a long time.
To avoid that these inactive members come to overload your customer database you can archive them.
Step 1: Archive a member
Step 1: Archive a member
Go to the member tab, on the line of a member click on the trash can icon.
You can also do it directly on the member's form by clicking on the icon at the bottom right.
The system will warn you if the member still has :
- unpaid invoices
unpaid invoices
- active cards
active cards
- future reservations or appointments
future reservations or appointments
- recurring reservations
recurring reservations
- current memberships
current memberships
- current auto-renewal memberships
current auto-renewal memberships
It is preferable to set these last elements before archiving a member
Once archived, the member will no longer appear in your member list or in the member search. However, the history of all these activities (course cards purchased, reservations, invoices...) will be kept. The member will have the indication "archived" next to its name.
Note: Archived members are not deleted, they are simply hidden. Therefore we keep the history of all their purchases and reservations on the platform and in the reports. The only reports where archived members are hidden are "Member" and "1st session (collective)".
Note:
Step 2: Search for an archived member
Step 2: Search for an archived member
To search for an archived member you can click on the search field and search for the name. The list of archived members appears at the bottom of the list of active members.
If you enter the member's email, number or exact name it will appear as a search suggestion.
Step 3: Filtering archived members with your smartlists
Step 3: Filtering archived members with your smartlists
Smartlists are by default filtered on active members, you can choose to filter them on archived members by clicking on a smartlist and in the default filter choose "Archived". You can also select all members.
Step 4: Unarchive a member
Step 4: Unarchive a member
To unarchive a member, go to the member's profile. You can access it using steps 2 and 3. On the member file, click on the green button at the bottom right.
Note: If the member is archived in the back office and the student reconnects to his account, the member will be automatically unarchived.

### Intercom
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/5355719-link-a-coupon-to-the-tags-owned-by-the-students

If you want certain type of members to benefit from a promotion, Bsport allows you to set up some coupons usable only by the members owning a certain type of tag.
1st Step : Create a tag on the member's profile.
Search fot the client's proifle > "My tags" > Click on "Create a category"  > Enter the name of the category and click on the little icon to save.
Create a tag associated to the category by clicking on "+" > Enter the name of the tag > Click on the icon to save it.
⚠️Warning, the category of the tag will appear on every member's profile but it doesn't mean that the tag is applied. You need to select it manually on every profile .
⚠️Warning, the category of the tag will appear on every member's profile but it doesn't mean that the tag is applied. You need to select it manually on every profile .
2nd Step : Create a coupon.
2nd Step :

### Intercom
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/4211090-merge-duplicated-member-accounts

It happens that you end up with duplicate customer records. This can happen when one and the same member registers twice with 2 different email addresses.
To merge two member accounts:
Go to the member account who must be deleted > Click on "Merge".
member account who must be deleted
Merge
Find the member whose account should be kept > Click on the arrow.
whose account should be kept
Verify the information and click on "Save" at the bottom of the page.
⚠️ Make sure to copy the following information from the member record that will be deleted to the member record that will be kept: First name, Last name, Sex, Phone number, Postal address. Without this, the merge will not work properly!!!⚠️
⚠️ Make sure to copy the following information from the member record that will be deleted to the member record that will be kept: First name, Last name, Sex, Phone number, Postal address. Without this, the merge will not work properly!!!
If the telephone number contains a 0 after the country code, this will not work. In this case, the 0 must be removed to obtain a + 44 7 XXX XXX XX format.

### Intercom
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/5316355-delete-the-payment-method-of-a-member

To facilitate the purchase of your members, Bsport offers you the possibility to save the payment methods.
This article will explain you to delete the payment method, in case of an expired card or following a client's request.
1st Step : Check the client's profile.
1st Step :
In the searchbar, write the name of the client > Click on it.
At the right bottom of the screen, the payment methods appear.
2nd Step : Delete the payment method.
2nd Step :

### Option 1: Using quick filters
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/6325057-filter-the-data-in-your-reports

Two options are available to filter the data in your reports:
- Quick filters: as the name suggests, this method is faster and requires fewer user inputs.
Quick filters: as the name suggests, this method is faster and requires fewer user inputs.
- Filtered views: this system is useful to easily switch between different filters, and offers more precise filtering thanks to and/or rules management.
Filtered views: this system is useful to easily switch between different filters, and offers more precise filtering thanks to and/or rules management.
Note that it is possible to apply both quick filters and a filtered view to the report. In this case, the items of the report would have to match both sets of filters.

## Option 1: Using quick filters

- Reporting > Select a report > Add a filter > Select which column you want to filter on.
Reporting > Select a report > Add a filter > Select which column you want to filter on.
- Configure the quick filter. The options available depend on the type of data for the selected column.
Configure the quick filter. The options available depend on the type of data for the selected column.
- Don't forget to generate your report again.
Don't forget to generate your report again.
generate your report
- You can add other quick filters to the first one. Note that the rule between filters will be "and": the filtered items will have to match all the filters. In the example below, the payments displayed will be dated 22/08 AND be of an amount under 50€.
You can add other quick filters to the first one. Note that the rule between filters will be "and": the filtered items will have to match all the filters. In the example below, the payments displayed will be dated 22/08 AND be of an amount under 50€.
and"
- A quick filter can be removed simply by clicking on the cross button and generating the report again.
A quick filter can be removed simply by clicking on the cross button and generating the report again.
Note: If you want to export the report, be aware that it will be filtered according to the quick filters!
Note

## Option 2: Using filtered views

Add filtered views to your reports to easily switch from one set of filters to another.
To add a filtered view:
- Report > Select a report > Add a filter > Add a filtered view.
Report > Select a report > Add a filter > Add a filtered view.
- Choose a name
Choose a name
- Choose a rule between filters: And / Or
Choose a rule between filters: And / Or
- And: The filtered items will have to match all the filters.
And: The filtered items will have to match all the filters.
And:
For example, if you are creating a report on payments, and want to filter out payments greater than or equal to 50 Euros AND having been made by credit card.
AND
This will only show you payments greater than 50 Euros that were made by credit card.
- Or: The filtered items should match at least one filter.
Or: The filtered items should match at least one filter.
For example, if you are reporting on payments, and want to filter out payments greater than 50 Euros OR payments with transaction fees greater than or equal to 5 Euros.
This will show you your payments greater than 50 Euros and your payments with transaction fees greater than or equal to 5 Euros.
- Next, select different elements to filter, according to the different columns of your report, and choose between a normal filter or a filter group.
Next, select different elements to filter, according to the different columns of your report, and choose between a normal filter or a filter group.
- A filter group allows you to apply a different rule and/or all filters within the group.
A filter group allows you to apply a different rule and/or all filters within the group.
For example, for a payment report, you can choose to create a filter that shows you all "data with a price greater than or equal to 50 Euros" And also having a "transaction fee greater than or equal to 5 Euros Or a payment method by Card, Cash, Check".

### Intercom
**Source:** https://intercom.help/bsport-helpcenter/en/articles/8320705-accrual-vs-cash-accounting

Cash and accrual accounting are two widely recognized approaches to managing your company's financial records. Understanding the distinction between these methods is crucial because they handle gift cards and internal account balances in contrasting ways. In this article, we'll explain the differences between these two methodologies and clarify why we opt for accrual-based accounting for our Purchases report.
Purchases
Consider this scenario:
On August 1st, a customer purchases a £100 gift card from your business. Subsequently, on August 18th, the customer's friend uses the gift card to purchase a £100 pass. At what point did your business generate revenue? The answer will be different depending on the method of accounting utilized.

## Cash-based accounting

Cash-based accounting
In cash-based accounting, revenue is recognized on the day your business receives payment through methods like cash, check, or credit. This means that when a purchase is made with a gift card or the internal account balance, there is no transaction to log because no actual cash has been received.
A cash-based financial report will reflect the revenue earned when a customer initially buys the gift card, but will not display the products or services subsequently acquired using that gift card.
In our example scenario, the £100 sale is recognized as revenue when the client purchases the gift card on August 1st, and no transaction is reported when the client's friend uses the gift card on August 18th.

## Accrual-based accounting

Accrual-based accounting
In accrual-based accounting, revenue is recognized on the day a sale is made, regardless of when the payment is received. This means that when a purchase is made with a gift card or the internal account balance, the business will recognize revenue on that day and not on the day the balance was credited, or the gift card was purchased.
An accrual-based financial report will not display the initial purchase of a gift card, but will reflect the revenue earned when a customer subsequently acquires products using that gift card.
In our example scenario, the £100 sale is recognized as revenue when the client's friend uses the gift card on August 18th, and no transaction is reported when the client purchases the gift card on August 1st.

## Why our Purchases report uses accrual-based accounting

When a business sells gift cards or offers account credit, accrual-based accounting is the recommended method. Since our platform provides both gift cards and an internal account balance, we use accrual-based accounting. Our Purchases report will not show the purchases of gift cards or the crediting of an internal account balance.
Purchases
If you need to specifically track the purchases of gift cards, our report Purchased gift cards can provide you with the necessary information.
Purchased gift cards
